# Agents · G · part 6

1,014 entries, glm-coordinator to God Agent Mnemosyne — Knowledge Agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **glm-coordinator**: 당신은 z.ai GLM-4.7 멀티에이전트 앙상블 시스템의 코디네이터입니다. ([m16khb/glm-ensemble](https://github.com/m16khb/glm-ensemble/blob/HEAD/agents/glm-coordinator.md))
- **glm-rescue**: Proactively use when Claude Code is stuck, wants a second implementation or diagnosis pass, needs a deeper root-cause investigation, or should hand a substantial coding task to GLM through the shared runtime ([hokanosekai/glm-plugin-cc](https://github.com/hokanosekai/glm-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/agents/glm-rescue.md))
- **glm-rescue**: Subagent that delegates active debugging, multi-file refactors, or implementation loops to GLM-5.3 via the glm plugin's broker. Use for tasks you want to run against GLM rather than the primary model. ([radityasurya/glm-plugin-cc](https://github.com/radityasurya/glm-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/plugins/glm/agents/glm-rescue.md))
- **glm-rescue**: Use for longer or more involved GLM delegations tracked as a background job with status/result lifecycle — versus the simple glm agent for quick one-shot questions. Trigger phrases: "/glm:rescue", "rescue", "GLM에 rescue 시켜", "백그라운드로 glm한테 시켜", "오래 걸리는 작업 glm한테 위임", "complex refactor with glm". ([yhzion/claude-plugin-models](https://github.com/yhzion/claude-plugin-models/blob/HEAD/plugins/glm/agents/glm-rescue.md))
- **glm-researcher**: Use for background research and code exploration tasks that should run on the GLM model. Investigates the codebase and reports findings. ([armenr/modelmux](https://github.com/armenr/modelmux/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/glm-researcher.md) · ★2)
- **glm-search-probe**: Проверены модели из фактического ответа `GET /v1/models` шлюза `https://airouter.softmg.tech/v1`: `opencode-go/glm-5.2`, `opencode-go/glm-5.3` и `opencode-go/glm-5.3-flash`. Контекст взят из трёх существующих неутверждённых черновиков; база открывалась только для чтения. Тексты, очередь и настройки провайдеров не менялись. ([softmg/otgolosok](https://github.com/softmg/otgolosok/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/glm-search-probe.md) · ★3)
- **glm-tester**: 只依据 odyssey 的 issue 与设计文档，在指定 worktree 写测试和独立参考实现。调用时给出 issue 号和 worktree 路径。 ([matrix72c/odyssey](https://github.com/matrix72c/odyssey/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/glm-tester.md))
- **glm-worker**: Worker agent that calls GLM API to perform a single task ([sungmanch/claude-glm-agent](https://github.com/sungmanch/claude-glm-agent/blob/HEAD/assets/agents/glm-worker.md) · ★5)
- **global**: - Use Australia/Melbourne for dates, schedules, and research windows. - Keep interaction warm, concise, and low in cognitive load. - Reply in one or two lines when that is enough. - Put one idea in each bullet. Use whitespace and small diagrams when they clarify structure. - Explain the reason when a decision, trade-off, or unfamiliar step matters. - Prefer clarity when terse wording would hide ri ([nathanvale/dotfiles-private](https://github.com/nathanvale/dotfiles-private/blob/HEAD/config/agents/global.md))
- **Global Podcast Strategist**: Expert podcast growth specialist focused on show positioning, audience development, content strategy, and monetisation. Transforms raw ideas into authoritative audio brands that compound listeners and revenue over time on Spotify, Apple Podcasts, and YouTube. ([criptogus/agent-evolve-network](https://github.com/criptogus/agent-evolve-network/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/marketing-global-podcast-strategist.md) · ★235 · 21 copies)
- **Global Podcast Strategist**: Ekspercki specjalista rozwoju podcastów, skoncentrowany na pozycjonowaniu programu, budowaniu audytorium, strategii treści i monetyzacji. Przekształca surowe pomysły w autorytatywne marki audio, które z czasem kumulują słuchaczy i przychody na Spotify, Apple Podcasts i YouTube. ([neurise-io/neurise-adseo](https://github.com/neurise-io/neurise-adseo/blob/HEAD/agents/marketing-global-podcast-strategist.md) · ★2)
- **global-agent**: Multi-line folded description for the fixture global agent — line-oriented parsers break here. ([atretyak1985/swarmery](https://github.com/atretyak1985/swarmery/blob/HEAD/tools/swarmery/testdata/sysconfig/claude/agents/global-agent.md) · ★9)
- **global-agents-md**: 共有正本は `config/agents/AGENTS.md`。`home/modules/agents.nix` が `~/.agents/AGENTS.md` と `~/.copilot/copilot-instructions.md` へ配る。 `home/modules/codex.nix` は同じ正本に Codex 固有の施行配線を加え、`~/.codex/AGENTS.md` へ配る。 Claude の固有指示は `~/.agents/AGENTS.md` を import する。決定の経緯・根拠は ADR-0032。 ([tarotene/dotfiles](https://github.com/tarotene/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/global-agents-md.md) · ★1)
- **global-aligner**: Check a unit, the spine, the skeleton or the assembled manuscript against the central claim, the gap, the results and the threads of evidence: serves the claim, stays inside the gap, opens or closes the right thread, uses only numbers that exist, and matches claim strength to design. Use in the SciFi paragraph loop for the global axis, at spine and skeleton gates, and for the whole-manuscript thread audit. ([Sdamirsa/SciFi](https://github.com/Sdamirsa/SciFi/blob/HEAD/agents/global-aligner.md) · ★2)

- **global-auditor**: 专利稿件 md 草稿的全局规则与短块审计员(多路审查之一)。权要一稿 / 全文一稿(含直写全文稿的权要闸门与全文闸门)的 md 草稿写完、进入 DOCX 前,以及权要返修/全文返修的增量复核轮,均与本阶段其他专审 auditor 并行调用。负责完整性一票否决、global.md 全局项(G3-G6)、全文术语一致性和短块规则;不写文件、不改 DOCX、不越权读取未传入的规则。 ([q1480371991/patent-harness](https://github.com/q1480371991/patent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/global-auditor.md) · ★1)
- **global-checker**: 多言語での意味・発音・国際展開の専門家。 brand-strategist が提案するアプリ名候補の国際展開可能性を評価する。 app-naming チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/marketing/app-naming/agents/global-checker.md) · ★38)
- **global-development**: Do not add `Co-Authored-By`, agent-session, or tool-attribution trailers to commit messages. Do not mention Claude, Anthropic, Codex, OpenAI, Gemini, or other agent tooling in commit messages, PR descriptions, or PR bodies. Commits should show only the human author unless repository-specific instructions say otherwise. In ([ruicore/codex-skills](https://github.com/ruicore/codex-skills/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/global-style.md) · ★2)

- **glossary**: Three defined terms, each with the one-line test for whether something is one. This file exists so that issues, ADRs and review surfaces can use the words precisely; it defines vocabulary and nothing else.
